Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # RBC_Count: million/uL adjustment\n    if feature == 'RBC_Count':\n        if value > 20:  # Might be in thousand/uL\n            return value / 1000.0\n        return value\n\n    # mg/L\n    'Aluminum': (0.0, 20.0),               # ug/L\n}\n\n\ndef clean_value(val):\n    \"\"\"Convert string value to numeric, handling scientific notation and comma decimals.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(val):\n        return np.nan\n\n    val_str = str(val).strip()\n    val_str = val_str.replace(',', '.')  # Convert comma to period\n\n    try:\n        return float(val_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef convert_feature(value, feature):\n    \"\"\"Apply unit conversion based on feature type.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 50:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Hematocrit: % conversion issues\n    if feature == 'Hematocrit':\n        if value > 100:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Albumin_Serum: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Albumin_Serum':\n        if value > 50:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Protein: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Protein':\n        if value > 100:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Prealbumin: mg/L -> mg/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Prealbumin':\n        if value > 500:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Creatinine: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Creatinine':\n        if value > 150:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n        return value\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'BUN':\n        if value > 50:  # Could be in mmol/L\n            return value / 2.8\n        return value\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Magnesium':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L (normal ~0.7-1.0 mmol/L)\n            return value / 2.43\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Ionized_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value > 6:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 3.1\n        return value\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Glucose':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 18.0\n        return value\n\n    # Uric_Acid: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.0168\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # LDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # HDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 88.57\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Direct_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Direct_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Iron: umol/L -> ug/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Iron':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # TIBC: umol/L -> ug/dL\n    if feature == 'TIBC':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # RBC_Count: million/uL adjustment\n    if feature == 'RBC_Count':\n        if value > 20:  # Might be in thousand/uL\n            return value / 1000.0\n        return value\n\n    # CRP: mg/L -> mg/L (no change needed typically)\n    # Already in expected units\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Step 1: Clean and convert all values\n    print(\"Step 1: Cleaning data - converting scientific notation and comma decimals...\")\n\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(clean_value)\n\n    print(f\"Data shape after cleaning: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # g/L to g/dL\n    'Total_Protein': ('g/L', 0.1),                 # g/L to g/dL\n    'Prealbumin': ('g/L', 0.1),                    # g/L to g/dL\n    'pH_Arterial': ('pH', 1),                      # same\n    'pCO2_Arterial': ('kPa', 7.5),                 # kPa to mmHg\n    'pO2_Arterial': ('kPa', 7.5),                  # kPa to mmHg\n}\n\n# Features where values might be in g/L (need to divide by 10)\nG_L_TO_G_DL = ['Hemoglobin', 'Hematocrit', 'Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein', 'Prealbumin']\n\n\ndef clean_value(val):\n    \"\"\"Convert string value to numeric, handling scientific notation and comma decimals.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(val):\n        return np.nan\n\n    val_str = str(val).strip()\n\n    # Handle scientific notation\n    val_str = val_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    # Try to parse as float\n    try:\n        return float(val_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef convert_si_to_conventional(row, feature, value):\n    \"\"\"Convert SI unit value to US conventional unit.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    if feature not in CONVERSION_FACTORS:\n        return value\n\n    si_unit, conversion_factor = CONVERSION_FACTORS[feature]\n    return value * conversion_factor\n\n\ndef needs_unit_conversion(value, feature):\n    \"\"\"Check if value is outside physiological range and might need conversion.\"\"\"\n    if feature not in PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES:\n        return False\n\n    min_val, max_val = PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES[feature]\n    return value < min_val or value > max_val * 3  # Allow for CKD patients with elevated values\n\n\ndef apply_unit_conversion(value, feature):\n    \"\"\"Apply unit conversion based on likely SI unit.\"\"\"\n    if feature in G_L_TO_G_DL and value > 50:  # Likely in g/L\n        return value / 10.0\n\n    if feature == 'Hemoglobin' and value > 200:  # Likely in g/L\n        return value / 10.0\n\n    if feature == 'Serum_Creatinine' and value > 15:  # Likely in umol/L\n        return value / 88.4\n\n    if feature == 'Magnesium' and value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 2.43\n\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium' and value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 4.0\n\n    if feature == 'Phosphorus' and value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 3.1\n\n    if feature == 'Glucose' and value > 30:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 18.0\n\n    if feature == 'Uric_Acid' and value > 20:  # Likely in umol/L\n        return value * 0.0168\n\n    if feature == 'Total_Cholesterol' and value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 38.67\n\n    if feature == 'LDL_Cholesterol' and value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 38.67\n\n    if feature == 'HDL_Cholesterol' and value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 38.67\n\n    if feature == 'Triglycerides' and value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n        return value / 88.57\n\n    if feature == 'Total_Bilirubin' and value > 10:  # Likely in umol/L\n        return value * 0.058\n\n    if feature == 'Direct_Bilirubin' and value > 5:  # Likely in umol/L\n        return value * 0.058\n\n    if feature == 'BUN' and value > 100:  # May be in mmol/L\n        return value / 2.8\n\n    if feature == 'Ferritin' and value > 1000:  # Sometimes in pmol/L\n        return value / 1.15\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Clean and convert all values\n    print(\"Step 1: Cleaning data - converting scientific notation and comma decimals...\")\n\n    # Create a copy of the dataframe\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    # Clean each column\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(clean_value)\n\n    print(f\"Data shape after cleaning: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with any missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    df_clean = df_clean.dropna()\n    print(f\"Data shape after removing missing values: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit conversions\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        # mg/L\n    'Aluminum': (0.0, 20.0),               # ug/L\n}\n\n\ndef clean_value(val):\n    \"\"\"Convert string value to numeric, handling scientific notation and comma decimals.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(val):\n        return np.nan\n\n    val_str = str(val).strip()\n    val_str = val_str.replace(',', '.')  # Convert comma to period\n\n    try:\n        return float(val_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef convert_feature(value, feature):\n    \"\"\"Apply unit conversion based on feature type.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 50:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Hematocrit: % conversion issues\n    if feature == 'Hematocrit':\n        if value > 100:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Albumin_Serum: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Albumin_Serum':\n        if value > 50:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Protein: g/L -> g/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Protein':\n        if value > 100:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Prealbumin: mg/L -> mg/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Prealbumin':\n        if value > 500:\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Creatinine: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Creatinine':\n        if value > 150:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n        return value\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'BUN':\n        if value > 50:  # Could be in mmol/L\n            return value / 2.8\n        return value\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Magnesium':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L (normal ~0.7-1.0 mmol/L)\n            return value / 2.43\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Ionized_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value > 6:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 3.1\n        return value\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Glucose':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 18.0\n        return value\n\n    # Uric_Acid: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.0168\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # LDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 15:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # HDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value > 20:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 88.57\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Total_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Direct_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL\n    if feature == 'Direct_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Iron: umol/L -> ug/dL\n    if feature == 'Serum_Iron':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # TIBC: umol/L -> ug/dL\n    if feature == 'TIBC':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # RBC_Count: million/uL adjustment\n    if feature == 'RBC_Count':\n        if value > 20:  # Might be in thousand/uL\n            return value / 1000.0\n        return value\n\n    # CRP: mg/L -> mg/L (no change needed typically)\n    # Already in expected units\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Step 1: Clean and convert all values\n    print(\"Step 1: Cleaning data - converting scientific notation and comma decimals...\")\n\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(clean_value)\n\n    print(f\"Data shape after cleaning: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit conversions\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: convert_feature(x, feature)\n            )\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].round(2)\n\n    # Verify and print statistics\n    print(\"\\nVerifying values within physiological ranges...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES:\n            min_val, max_val = PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES[feature]\n            values = df_clean[feature]\n            min_actual = values.min()\n            max_actual = values.max()\n\n            # mg/L\n    'Aluminum': (0.0, 100.0),              # ug/L\n}\n\n\ndef clean_value(val):\n    \"\"\"Convert string value to numeric, handling scientific notation and comma decimals.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(val):\n        return np.nan\n\n    val_str = str(val).strip()\n    val_str = val_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        return float(val_str)\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef convert_feature(value, feature):\n    \"\"\"Apply unit conversion based on feature type.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return value\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 25:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Albumin_Serum: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Albumin_Serum':\n        if value > 7:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Protein: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Total_Protein':\n        if value > 14:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 2.8)\n    # Normal BUN is 7-20 mg/dL = 2.5-7.1 mmol/L urea\n    if feature == 'BUN':\n        if value > 40:  # Could be in mmol/L (urea)\n            return value / 2.8\n        return value\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 2.43)\n    # Normal Mg is 1.7-2.2 mg/dL = 0.7-0.9 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Magnesium':\n        if value > 3:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 2.43\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    # Normal Ca is 8.5-10.5 mg/dL = 2.1-2.6 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Ionized_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    if feature == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 3.1)\n    # Normal P is 2.5-4.5 mg/dL = 0.8-1.5 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value > 8:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 3.1\n        return value\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 18.0)\n    # Normal glucose is 70-100 mg/dL = 3.9-5.6 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Glucose':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 18.0\n        return value\n\n    # Uric_Acid: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.0168)\n    # Normal uric acid is 3.4-7.0 mg/dL = 202-416 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.0168\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    # Normal cholesterol is < 200 mg/dL = < 5.2 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Total_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 15:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 5, so > 15 suggests mmol/L or error\n            # Actually looking at distribution, values are mostly in mg/dL\n            # So we don't convert\n            return value\n        return value\n\n    # LDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    if feature == 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 10:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 2.6\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # HDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    if feature == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 5:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 1.3\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 88.57)\n    # Normal TG is < 150 mg/dL = < 1.7 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value > 15:  # Values > 15 suggest mmol/L (would be > 1327 mg/dL)\n            return value / 88.57\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.058)\n    # Normal bilirubin is 0.1-1.2 mg/dL = 1.7-21 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Total_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Direct_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.058)\n    if feature == 'Direct_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 2:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Iron: umol/L -> ug/dL (multiply by 5.58)\n    # Normal serum iron is 60-150 ug/dL = 11-27 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Serum_Iron':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # TIBC: umol/L -> ug/dL (multiply by 5.58)\n    # Normal TIBC is 250-370 ug/dL = 45-66 umol/L\n    if feature == 'TIBC':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # RBC_Count: check for units\n    if feature == 'RBC_Count':\n        if value > 50:  # Might be in thousand/uL instead of million/uL\n            return value / 1000.0\n        return value\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with any missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    df_clean = df_clean.dropna()\n    print(f\"Data shape after removing missing values: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit conversions\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: convert_feature(x, feature)\n            )\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].round(2)\n\n    # Verify and print statistics\n    print(\"\\nVerifying values within physiological ranges...\")\n\n    issues = []\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES:\n            min_val, max_val = PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES[feature]\n            values = df_clean[feature]\n            min_actual = values.min()\n            max_actual = values.max()\n\n            # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Protein: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Total_Protein':\n        if value > 14:  # Likely in g/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Prealbumin: mg/L -> mg/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Prealbumin':\n        if value > 80:  # Likely in mg/L\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Creatinine: umol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 88.4)\n    # Normal range is 0.6-1.2 mg/dL = 53-106 umol/L\n    # In CKD, can be much higher\n    if feature == 'Serum_Creatinine':\n        if value > 100:  # Likely in umol/L (values > 100 umol/L need conversion)\n            return value / 88.4\n        return value\n\n    # BUN: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 2.8)\n    # Normal BUN is 7-20 mg/dL = 2.5-7.1 mmol/L urea\n    if feature == 'BUN':\n        if value > 40:  # Could be in mmol/L (urea)\n            return value / 2.8\n        return value\n\n    # Magnesium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 2.43)\n    # Normal Mg is 1.7-2.2 mg/dL = 0.7-0.9 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Magnesium':\n        if value > 3:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 2.43\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    # Normal Ca is 8.5-10.5 mg/dL = 2.1-2.6 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Ionized_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    if feature == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Phosphorus: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 3.1)\n    # Normal P is 2.5-4.5 mg/dL = 0.8-1.5 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Phosphorus':\n        if value > 8:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 3.1\n        return value\n\n    # Glucose: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 18.0)\n    # Normal glucose is 70-100 mg/dL = 3.9-5.6 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Glucose':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 18.0\n        return value\n\n    # Uric_Acid: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.0168)\n    # Normal uric acid is 3.4-7.0 mg/dL = 202-416 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Uric_Acid':\n        if value > 30:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.0168\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    # Normal cholesterol is < 200 mg/dL = < 5.2 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Total_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 15:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 5, so > 15 suggests mmol/L or error\n            # Actually looking at distribution, values are mostly in mg/dL\n            # So we don't convert\n            return value\n        return value\n\n    # LDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    if feature == 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 10:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 2.6\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # HDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 38.67)\n    if feature == 'HDL_Cholesterol':\n        if value > 5:  # Values in mmol/L would be > 1.3\n            return value / 38.67\n        return value\n\n    # Triglycerides: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 88.57)\n    # Normal TG is < 150 mg/dL = < 1.7 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Triglycerides':\n        if value > 15:  # Values > 15 suggest mmol/L (would be > 1327 mg/dL)\n            return value / 88.57\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.058)\n    # Normal bilirubin is 0.1-1.2 mg/dL = 1.7-21 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Total_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Direct_Bilirubin: umol/L -> mg/dL (multiply by 0.058)\n    if feature == 'Direct_Bilirubin':\n        if value > 2:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 0.058\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Iron: umol/L -> ug/dL (multiply by 5.58)\n    # Normal serum iron is 60-150 ug/dL = 11-27 umol/L\n    if feature == 'Serum_Iron':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # TIBC: umol/L -> ug/dL (multiply by 5.58)\n    # Normal TIBC is 250-370 ug/dL = 45-66 umol/L\n    if feature == 'TIBC':\n        if value > 50:  # Likely in umol/L\n            return value * 5.58\n        return value\n\n    # RBC_Count: check for units\n    if feature == 'RBC_Count':\n        if value > 50:  # Might be in thousand/uL instead of million/uL\n            return value / 1000.0\n        return value\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Step 1: Clean and convert all values\n    print(\"Step 1: Cleaning data - converting scientific notation and comma decimals...\")\n\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(clean_value)\n\n    print(f\"Data shape after cleaning: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with any missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    df_clean = df_clean.dropna()\n    print(f\"Data shape after removing missing values: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit conversions\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: convert_feature(x, feature)\n            )\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].round(2)\n\n    # Verify and print statistics\n    print(\"\\nVerifying values within physiological ranges...\")\n\n    issues = []\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES:\n            min_val, max_val = PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES[feature]\n            values = df_clean[feature]\n            min_actual = values.min()\n            max_actual = values.max()\n\n            # Check if values are still wildly out of range\n            if min_actual < min_val * 0.3 or max_actual > max_val * 3:\n                issues.append(f\"  {feature}: min={min_actual:.2f}, max={max_actual:.2f} (expected: {min_val}-{max_val})\")\n\n    if issues:\n        print(\"Potential issues (values still outside expected range):\")\n        for issue in issues:\n            print(issue)\n    else:\n        print(\"All values within expected physiological ranges!\")\n\n    # Based on distribution analysis, only convert features that are clearly in SI units\n\n    # Hemoglobin: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Hemoglobin':\n        if value > 25:  # Clearly in g/L if > 25\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Albumin_Serum: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Albumin_Serum':\n        if value > 7:  # Clearly in g/L if > 7\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Total_Protein: g/L -> g/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Total_Protein':\n        if value > 14:  # Clearly in g/L if > 14\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Prealbumin: mg/L -> mg/dL (divide by 10)\n    if feature == 'Prealbumin':\n        if value > 80:  # Clearly in mg/L if > 80\n            return value / 10.0\n        return value\n\n    # Likely in umol/L\n            return value / 88.4\n        return value\n\n    # Serum_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    # Normal Ca is 8.5-10.5 mg/dL = 2.1-2.6 mmol/L\n    if feature == 'Serum_Calcium':\n        if value > 10:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    # Ionized_Calcium: mmol/L -> mg/dL (divide by 4.0)\n    if feature == 'Ionized_Calcium':\n        if value > 5:  # Likely in mmol/L\n            return value / 4.0\n        return value\n\n    return value\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Step 1: Clean and convert all values\n    print(\"Step 1: Cleaning data - converting scientific notation and comma decimals...\")\n\n    df_clean = df.copy()\n\n    for col in df_clean.columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(clean_value)\n\n    print(f\"Data shape after cleaning: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 2: Remove rows with any missing values\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    df_clean = df_clean.dropna()\n    print(f\"Data shape after removing missing values: {df_clean.shape}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Apply unit conversions\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Converting mixed units to US conventional units...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].apply(\n                lambda x: convert_feature(x, feature)\n            )\n\n    # Step 4: Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature != 'patient_id':\n            df_clean[feature] = df_clean[feature].round(2)\n\n    # Verify and print statistics\n    print(\"\\nVerifying values within physiological ranges...\")\n\n    issues = []\n    for feature in df_clean.columns:\n        if feature == 'patient_id':\n            continue\n\n        if feature in PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES:\n            min_val, max_val = PHYSIOLOGICAL_RANGES[feature]\n            values = df_clean[feature]\n            min_actual = values.min()\n            max_actual = values.max()\n\n            # Check if values are still wildly out of range\n            if min_actual < min_val * 0.5 or max_actual > max_val * 2:\n                issues.append(f\"  {feature}: min={min_actual:.2f}, max={max_actual:.2f} (expected: {min_val}-{max_val})\")\n\n    if issues:\n        print(\"Features with values outside expected range:\")\n        for issue in issues:\n            print(issue)\n    else:\n        print(\"All values within expected physiological ranges!\")\n\n    #
